# Deliverables ledger

One row per PR-sized deliverable. **Every PR updates its own row in the same
PR.** Status values: planned / in progress / in review / merged. This file is
tracked externally — it must always reflect actual state.

**QA rule (2026-09-02):** a deliverable merged from the `qa` branch carries a
`-QA` suffix on its ID (e.g. `D44-QA`) here, in `docs/feature-status.md`'s
table and wherever the ID is cited; the plain D-sequence is reserved for the
planned deliverables and a QA row never reuses or blocks a number.

**Split-ID rule (2026-09-04):** when two deliverables have ended up sharing a
number, they take `-A` / `-B` suffixes in merge order (`D77-A` is PR #107,
`D77-B` is PR #108) rather than one being renumbered - a merged row's ID is
already cited in commits, PR descriptions and code comments, so moving it
breaks more than it fixes. Suffixed rows are updated everywhere the ID is
cited, exactly like the `-QA` rule above, and the shared number is not reused
again.

**Maintenance note (QA, 2026-09-02):** migration 014 was restored to its
recorded immutable contents after a digest mismatch. The tombstone-collision
repair lives in append-only migration 019; this correction does not introduce
a new deliverable.

**D66 follow-up (QA, 2026-09-02):** the LLM modal now uses stacked race cards
with collapsible entries, fixed header/footer controls, a generation spinner,
and next-race navigation that advances from the race most recently saved. It
also shows payout estimates in preview, displays generation/save errors in the
affected race card, preserves model reasoning in saved allocations and keeps
that reasoning visible at the preview's compact size. The LLM prompt receives
stored external consensus, including Sports from the Basement.

| D348 | Fix: Equibase OTR confirm 500'd (and saved nothing) when the sheet printed more races than the day had on file | - | PR [#349](https://github.com/KevinRaffay/betsheet/pull/349), branch `claude/otr-card-confirm-500-c61cf3` | merged | **Bug report**: "Unable to confirm and save three cards after uploading OTR file" - `500 (Internal Server Error)` on `POST /api/race-days/291/equibase-otr/confirm`. Reproduced by copying the real (read-only) `data/betsheet.sqlite` and `data/archive/equibase-otr/GP/2026-09-11.pdf` into a scratch dir and re-running the actual upload -> preview -> confirm path against the copy (never the real files, which were left untouched - `manifest.json`'s `confirmed_sha256` for that day is still absent). Race day 291 is Gulfstream Park 2026-09-11 with 9 races on file (`entries_source: 'equibase_html'`), but its archived Off to the Races sheet prints 15 races (`Race 1:` through `Race 15:` - GP evidently ran a doubleheader/twilight card that day and entries ingestion only captured the main 9). `confirmEquibaseOtr` (`server/equibase-otr.js`) built `raceIdByNumber` from the day's 9 races and then looped over all of `parsed.perRace` unconditionally; for race 10-15's tickets, `raceIdByNumber[r.race]` was `undefined`, and passing that to `allocations`' NOT NULL `race_id` column threw `SqliteError: NOT NULL constraint failed: allocations.race_id` inside the transaction - uncaught (not an `EquibaseOtrError`), so the route fell through to a bare 500 and the transaction rolled back, meaning NONE of the three cards were saved, not even for the 9 races that matched fine. **Fixed in `parseAgainstDay`** (shared by both preview and confirm): a parsed race number the day doesn't have on file is now dropped before ticket construction, with a new non-blocking `otr_unmatched_race` warning per dropped race - the same "warn and skip the race, never the whole day" contract `otr_unparsed_race` already uses for a race that couldn't be read at all, so a sheet that overruns the day's races now confirms cleanly for every race that DOES match. **VERIFIED**: `npm run check-equibase-otr` green (new section 7b: a synthetic day seeded with only races 1-6 against a real 8-race fixture PDF - preview flags races 7/8 as `otr_unmatched_race`, confirm returns 201 with three cards, the "both" card carries exactly 24 tickets (4/race x 6), none for the missing races); `npm run build` exit 0. The original repro (against the copied real db/archive, never the committed ones) confirmed the fix turns the 500 into a 201 with three real cards for race day 291's own 9 races, `blockingCount: 12` (the pre-existing, correctly-handled `unknown_program` warnings on races 2/4/5/6 - unrelated to this bug and unaffected by the fix). |
| D345 | Deploy workflow: trigger the Pages deploy from `deploy-static-app`, not `main` | 4 | PR [#346](https://github.com/KevinRaffay/betsheet/pull/346), branch `claude/deploy-static-app-branch` | merged | **User request**: stop the recurring, data-only "publish a snapshot" commits from landing on `main`, which this repo's delivery workflow (CLAUDE.md) reserves for one-deliverable-per-PR code changes. D336 already made publishing a snapshot a routine UI action ("Publish snapshot" on the race day list) that writes `static/public/payload.json` locally but never commits/pushes itself - the person still has to commit and push by hand to trigger a deploy, and until now that push had to land on `main` because `.github/workflows/deploy-pages.yml` only triggered on `push: branches: [main]`. Changed the trigger to `branches: [deploy-static-app]` instead. `main`'s pre-push hook (`scripts/git-hooks/pre-push`) only ever blocked `main`/`master`, so `deploy-static-app` takes a direct `git push` with no PR required - exactly the low-ceremony path a frequent, reviewable-only-by-looking-at-the-data snapshot commit should have, while `main` stays PR-gated and free of them. The branch is not required to track `main` 1:1: pick up code changes by merging or fast-forwarding from `main` whenever needed, then push the payload commit on top - `workflow_dispatch` still re-runs a deploy against whatever the branch currently holds. Created the `deploy-static-app` branch itself (from `main`'s new tip, after #346 merged) so the trigger has something to fire on - `git branch -f deploy-static-app origin/main && git push origin deploy-static-app`. `docs/architecture-map.md` and `docs/commands.md` updated for the new trigger and the recommended push target. **VERIFIED, with one honest gap**: workflow YAML change only (a single `branches:` value), no code touched - `npm run build` unaffected and not re-run for this; confirmed `main`'s own last push (the #346 merge itself) did NOT trigger a deploy run, since the workflow file that landed with that push already says `deploy-static-app`, matching the design intent exactly. **Could not confirm the branch's own creation push fired a run**: `GET /actions/runs?branch=deploy-static-app` showed zero runs after the push, and a manual `workflow_dispatch` to double-check was refused by `scripts/gh-api.js`'s own allowlist (deliberately - it permits reads/PRs/merges and nothing that changes repository/Action state, D217/D218) rather than worked around. Plausible, undiagnosed cause: GitHub's documented behavior for a `push`-with-`paths` trigger on a **branch-creation** event (no prior commits on that ref to diff against) may not evaluate the same way an ordinary push does. This will resolve itself the first time someone actually pushes a real payload commit to `deploy-static-app` (the intended use), or can be checked directly from the Actions tab's "Run workflow" button - flagging it here rather than claiming a verification that didn't actually happen. |
| D342 | `.gitattributes`: keep `static/public/payload.json` untouched by CRLF normalization | 4 | PR [#343](https://github.com/KevinRaffay/betsheet/pull/343), branch `claude/gitattributes-static-payload-lf` | merged | User reported the Git warning `LF will be replaced by CRLF the next time Git touches it` on `static/public/payload.json`, seen after D337's fix let the builder actually write the file. Root cause: `scripts/build-static-payload.js` writes the file with plain `\n` newlines (Node's `fs.writeFileSync` adds none of its own), while `core.autocrlf=true` on Windows normalizes tracked text files to CRLF on checkout - harmless to the JSON content itself, but it would make every regenerated payload diff as fully changed, the same problem `tests/fixtures/**` and `archive/**` already carry `-text` for. Added `static/public/payload.json -text` alongside those two existing entries, same reasoning, same fix shape - a generated data file is not source text a human edits, so Git should leave its bytes alone rather than translate them. Did not touch the currently-committed file's bytes (already CRLF throughout, 2699 lines, checked directly) - `-text` freezes it as-is going forward, exactly as the existing two entries' own comments describe doing for their files, so this is attribute-only with no content diff. |
| D337 | Fix static-payload validator: a scratched entry's missing program_number aborted the ENTIRE build | 4 | PR [#338](https://github.com/KevinRaffay/betsheet/pull/338), branch `claude/build-static-payload-missing-files-a96809` | merged | Reported live: `npm run build-static-payload -- --from 2026-06-01 --to 2026-12-31` printed ~20 warnings ("an entry has no program_number") and wrote no file at all - `buildStaticPayload` is all-or-nothing, so one bad entry anywhere in the requested range fails the whole bundle. Root cause: `shared/static-payload.js`'s `validateDay` required every entry's `program_number` to be a non-empty string, unconditionally - but migration 032 (D180) made the column NULLable specifically because some Equibase scratch rows print no number at all, and `CardSheet.jsx`/`EntriesTable.jsx` already render that case as `—`. Checked the real corpus directly (read-only, against `data/betsheet.sqlite`): 226 entries carry a null `program_number`, all 226 scratched, none live - exactly the D180 shape, confirming the validator was the bug, not the data. Fixed to accept `program_number: null` only when `scratched === true` (a null on a non-scratched entry is still refused, as corruption); also stopped counting nulls as duplicate program numbers (`Set.has(null)` would have flagged the second scratch in any race with two). **A second, unrelated bug blocked even claiming a D-number for this fix**: `npm run allocate-deliverable` 422'd on every call after the label's first-ever creation, because `ensureDeliverableLabel` (`scripts/lib/deliverable-issues.js`, D324) matched `already_exists` against the thrown error's top-level `message` - but GitHub's real 422 body puts that code in `errors[].code` and leaves `message` as the generic "Validation Failed", so the match could never fire. `check-deliverable-issues.js`'s own fixture encoded the same wrong assumption (`{message: 'already_exists'}`, a shape GitHub never actually sends) - the D201 lesson recurring: a check only proves what its fixture resembles. Fixed by folding `errors[]` into the thrown message and adding a real-shape fixture as a regression control; claiming this very D-number (issue [#337](https://github.com/KevinRaffay/betsheet/issues/337)) is the live proof it works. **VERIFIED**: `npm run check-static-payload` (two new cases: a scratched null-`program_number` entry validates and two such entries in one race are not flagged as duplicates) and `npm run check-deliverable-issues` (new real-shape regression case), both green; `build-static-payload.js` re-run against the real corpus for `--from 2026-06-01 --to 2026-12-31` (read-only, `BETSHEET_LOG_DIR` redirected to a scratch dir so no real log file was touched) now writes the bundle for every matching day. |
| D336 | Publish the static snapshot from the UI, not just the CLI | 4 | PR [#340](https://github.com/KevinRaffay/betsheet/pull/340), branch `claude/ecstatic-mayer-up4s0g` | merged | **User request 2026-09-11**, following directly from a question about why the deployed static viewer only showed 2 Del Mar days (answer: the committed payload was a Phase 3 verification fixture, never rebuilt from the real corpus) and whether the build could be triggered without a terminal. **New**: `client/src/components/PublishStaticSnapshotModal.jsx`, opened by a "Publish snapshot" button on `RaceDayList.jsx`'s pagehead row. Checkbox-selects days (WYSIWYG over the CLI's `--from/--to/--track` resolution - the request always carries the exact `dayIds` the checkboxes show checked, never a date range re-resolved server-side, so what you selected can never disagree with what gets published), reusing the SAME active (non-deleted) day list `RaceDayList` already loaded - invariant 12's soft-delete exclusion is inherited for free. Posts to new `POST /api/static-payload/publish` (`server/static-payload-route.js`), which accepts `{dayIds}` XOR `{from, to, track}` (mirroring the CLI's own mutual exclusion), writes `static/public/payload.json`, and logs an app-level `static_payload_published` audit event (mirroring `app_reset`'s shape - not a card-session event, since it is not scoped to one card's own trace, invariant 8). **The UI states plainly, before and after publishing, that this only writes a file on this machine** - it never commits, pushes, or deploys, and a Pages site is publicly readable even from a private repo, so nothing here should be pushed without meaning to make those days' cards and grades public. **Refactor to avoid a dependency cycle, not incidental**: `scripts/build-static-payload.js` already imported `server/cards.js`/`server/grading.js`/`server/db.js`, and this codebase's `server/` never imports from `scripts/` anywhere - so the route could not simply `import` the script without inverting that direction. Fixed the same way D329 already fixed the layer below it (`getCardCore`/`getDayRaces`/`getDayFooter`/`getCardGrades`, extracted so both a route and a script call them): the buildable logic (`buildStaticDay`/`resolveDayIds`/`buildStaticPayload`/`payloadHashOf`/`summarizePayload`/`DEFAULT_OUT`) moved into new `server/static-payload-builder.js`, and `scripts/build-static-payload.js` shrank to argument parsing, console reporting and file-writing around it - byte-identical CLI behavior, confirmed by `check-static-payload`'s existing subprocess test passing unmodified. New `BETSHEET_STATIC_PAYLOAD_OUT` env var (mirroring `BETSHEET_DB`/`BETSHEET_LOG_DIR`) overrides `DEFAULT_OUT` for check-script use only - the route itself accepts no client-supplied output path, so a browser can never choose an arbitrary file to write on this machine. **`scripts/check-static-app.js` had a stale-check bug found and fixed in the same pass**: its "the payload builder still emits no `program_rank`" assertion read `scripts/build-static-payload.js`, which after this extraction no longer contains any entry-building code at all - it would have passed vacuously forever, exactly the D201/D205 class of a check whose target moved out from under it. Repointed at `server/static-payload-builder.js`, where that code actually lives now. **VERIFIED**: new `scripts/check-publish-static-payload.js` (added to `package.json`) boots a real server on a temp DB with `BETSHEET_STATIC_PAYLOAD_OUT` redirected, seeds two real race days and a graded HUMAN card over HTTP, and drives the route exactly as the UI does - both request forms (`dayIds` and `from/to/track`) resolving the same days, every refusal shape (neither/both request forms, an unknown day, a soft-deleted day, a range matching nothing) returning the right status without writing a file, the written file validating against the schema, and the app-log audit event. **Carries its own negative control**: reads the real committed `static/public/payload.json` before and after the entire run and asserts it is byte-identical, proving the env-var redirect actually took effect rather than trusting it silently. `npm run check-static-payload` (unmodified, confirms the CLI extraction is behavior-preserving), `npm run check-static-app` (with the repointed assertion), `npm run check-module-bindings`, `npm run build` all green. **Browser-verified end to end** (Playwright + bundled Chromium, agent's `dev:preview` stack, never the human's pair): seeded 2 real Del Mar days plus one graded card over HTTP, opened the race day list, clicked "Publish snapshot", selected both days via the header checkbox, clicked "Publish selected (2)", and confirmed the result panel showed both days with the correct card/graded counts and hashes; the manually-triggered write to the REAL `static/public/payload.json` was confirmed via `git diff` and then reverted, since it was test data rather than a deliberate publish of the corpus. Zero `#root`-breaking errors; the one console 404 seen is pre-existing/unrelated (confirmed by reproducing it on a plain page load with none of this PR's code exercised). |
| D332 | Static app read-only rework, phase 4 of 4: final docs consistency pass | 4 | PR [#333](https://github.com/KevinRaffay/betsheet/pull/333), branch `claude/ecstatic-mayer-up4s0g` | merged | **Phase 4 of the static-app read-only rework** (D236 phase 1, D237 phase 2, D329 phase 3) - assessed honestly against the approved plan rather than padded with invented scope, since the plan itself said this phase "may fold into Phase 3 entirely" if that phase's own doc updates were thorough. **They were, mostly**: `.github/workflows/deploy-pages.yml`'s header comments were already rewritten for the multi-day bundle model in D329's own commit, and `CLAUDE.md`/`docs/architecture-map.md`/`docs/commands.md`/`docs/feature-status.md` were all updated there too. **One genuine loose end survived a repo-wide sweep** (`grep -rn "reference.cards\|referenceCards\|TicketBuilder"` across every live doc): `REQUIREMENTS.md`'s "Static Pages target: build HUMAN cards at the track" section - a live, maintained requirements-to-deliverable map, not a frozen snapshot - still presented the now-DELETED card-construction spec (browser ticket builder, IndexedDB drafts, export/import, `--reference-cards` reveal-and-stamp) as the current app, and still asserted the retired "one deliberate exception to benchmark first, bet later" framing as present-tense fact, directly contradicting `CLAUDE.md`'s own rewritten opening-rule paragraph from Phase 1. Fixed with a superseded-marker paragraph at the top of that section (mirroring the treatment `docs/feature-status.md`'s 2026-09-08 scope note got in D236) pointing at CLAUDE.md's rewrite and at D236/D237/D329's own ledger rows for what the current app does - the historical requirements table itself is left untouched as the record of what D150-D158 built. **Everything else checked and found already consistent**: `AGENTS.md` is a long-stale, unrelated mirror of an old `CLAUDE.md` (last touched for whitespace only, predates far more than this rework) and fixing it would be unrelated scope creep well beyond this task; `docs/trace-schema.md`'s `static_card_imported` event was already correctly marked historical in Phase 1; every other `TicketBuilder`/`reference-cards` hit in a live doc is either about the still-live DESKTOP `TicketBuilder.jsx` component (unrelated to the static app) or already-correct historical ledger prose. **VERIFIED**: documentation-only change (`git diff --stat` is `REQUIREMENTS.md` plus this row), no code, schema, or behavior touched, so no check script applies; `npm run build` and `npm run build:static` were already re-confirmed green in Phase 3's own commit and nothing here could move them. |
| D329 | Static app: payload schema v4, multi-day builder, read-only viewer (phase 3 of 4) | 4 | PR [#330](https://github.com/KevinRaffay/betsheet/pull/330), branch `claude/ecstatic-mayer-up4s0g` | merged | **Phase 3 of the static-app read-only rework** (D236 phase 1, D237 phase 2) - the largest phase: redesigns the payload schema to bundle multiple race days, makes every card on a day (with its grades) part of the payload unconditionally, and builds the calendar/list/day/race/card viewer on top of it. **Claimed under the NEW GitHub-issue allocator (D324)**, which lands on D329 rather than a continuation of D237/D238 - by the time this branch synced with `origin/main`, D240/D241/D324 had all merged from concurrent sessions (D240 itself a renumber of a collided D236 claim), so the shared issue/PR counter had moved well past D238. Every internal `D238` reference this phase's own files had accumulated before that sync was renamed to `D329` in one pass, confirmed against a repo-wide grep that D238 appears nowhere else. **Schema v4** (`shared/static-payload.js`): top level is `{schema, schemaVersion: 4, generatedAt, raceDays: [...]}`; `payloadHash` is now PER DAY, scoped to `{schema, schemaVersion, raceDay, races}` only (cards/grades excluded, so a regrade or a new note never moves it, and a day's hash is provably the same built alone or bundled). Entries stay snake_case; **cards/tickets/allocations/grades are ALSO snake_case**, matching `client/src/components/CardSheet.jsx` (D237) exactly, so the viewer reuses that component with zero translation layer - discovered mid-implementation to be the right shape rather than imposing a camelCase layer on top of it. **Server extraction, not a second copy of the shape**: `server/cards.js` gains `getCardCore`/`getDayRaces`/`getDayFooter` and `server/grading.js` gains `getCardGrades`, factored out of the existing `GET /api/cards/:id` and `GET /api/cards/:id/grades` routes (which now just compose them, verified byte-identical to the pre-refactor response) so `scripts/build-static-payload.js` calls the SAME functions the live API does rather than hand-rolling a second version of the card shape. **`build-static-payload.js`** now takes `<raceDayId> [<raceDayId> ...] [--out <path>]` or `--from <date> --to <date> [--track "..."]`; every card on a requested day is included, no more `--reference-cards` flag and no more HUMAN-card exclusion - the whole point of a read-only viewer is to show the cards that exist. `llm_model_label` is resolved at build time from `server/anthropic-client.js`'s `KNOWN_MODELS` table (never imported into the browser bundle). A soft-deleted day refuses the WHOLE bundle (invariant 12). **New viewer, hash-routed** (`static/src/app.jsx`): `#/` -> `DayList.jsx` (new, mirrors `RaceDayList.jsx`'s filter pattern), `#/calendar` -> `Calendar.jsx` (new, mirrors `RaceDayCalendar.jsx`'s tracks x Pacific-hour matrix via `shared/race-calendar.js`), `#/day/:id` -> `DayView.jsx` (repurposed, gains a Cards table), `#/day/:id/race/:n` -> `RaceView.jsx` (repurposed, route-aware of the day), `#/day/:id/card/:id` -> `CardView.jsx` (new, static-app's own, thin wrapper around `CardSheet.jsx` sourced from the in-memory bundle, no fetch). **Import surface grows from three to FIVE** (re-derived per the D168 house rule): `shared/static-payload.js`, `client/src/styles.css`, `client/src/components/EntriesTable.jsx` (unchanged from D236), plus `client/src/components/CardSheet.jsx` and `shared/race-calendar.js` (new). `CardSheet.jsx`'s own transitive imports (`RaceNotes.jsx`, `EntryFlagTags.jsx`, `shared/entry-flags.js`, `shared/card-notices.js`) are reached only through it, not directly from any `static/src/` file, so they are not separate entries on the list. **`check-static-app.js`**: dropped the parser-round-trip section entirely (nothing under `static/src/` imports `human-picks.js` any more since D236); added two positive controls for genuinely new code (`outcome outcome--`, CardSheet's grading display; `Race day calendar`, the new nav) alongside the unchanged six forbidden-marker negative controls and the grading-computation control (display of an already-computed grade is not the same as shipping the grader, and a comment now says so). **`.github/workflows/deploy-pages.yml`**: trigger paths grow to cover `CardSheet.jsx`/`RaceNotes.jsx`/`EntryFlagTags.jsx`/`shared/entry-flags.js`/`shared/card-notices.js`/`shared/race-calendar.js`; the deploy-gate step logs each bundled day's track/date/race count/card count/graded count and states plainly that every card and grade on a bundled day is now public (no more conditional `--reference-cards` warning). **`CLAUDE.md` invariant 10 rewritten**: a bundled day's entries, cards and grades are exactly what belongs in a payload now; the privacy floor narrows to nothing about the person who ran the builder. D168's gotcha re-derived to FIVE places, naming the two new ones. `docs/architecture-map.md`/`docs/commands.md` updated for the new schema, builder CLI, and viewer files. **VERIFIED**: `npm run check-static-payload` (rewritten for v4 - two real fixture days through the real writer, per-day hash stability including bundling-invariance and regrade-invariance, a soft-deleted day refusing the whole bundle, `--from/--to` resolving the same set as explicit ids, ~30 named refusal cases in snake_case, the CLI run as a real subprocess - all green), `npm run check-static-app` (rewritten, all green), `npm run check-grading` and `npm run check-ingest` (unmodified, confirm the `server/cards.js`/`server/grading.js` extraction didn't change route behavior, both green), `npm run build` and `npm run build:static` (exit 0). **Browser pass at `:5187`** (Playwright + bundled Chromium) through the committed two-day fixture bundle: day list with date/track filters, calendar matrix, a day view showing its races and one graded card with `+$52` P/L, a race's entries table, the card sheet itself (HUMAN chip, bankroll math, per-race allocations, the graded summary and outcome tags, all matching the seeded data), and the "no such day" fallback for an unknown id - zero console/page errors across every route, `#root` intact throughout. |
| D324 | GitHub Issues replace the SQLite D-number allocator; `npm run gh` grows issue subcommands | - | PR [#327](https://github.com/KevinRaffay/betsheet/pull/327), branch `claude/github-projects-issue-tracking-de6626` | merged | **User request**: investigate GitHub Projects for issue tracking and deliverable IDs, and "finally resolve this D ID mess" - the three-deliverable saga (D203/D226/D230) of local SQLite coordination that never fully closed the cross-clone race, most recently documented failing again live: D237 was claimed by an unrelated concurrent session while this investigation was in progress, caught by `syncCounterFloor`'s own remote-ledger check the moment this branch fetched origin. **Investigated GitHub Projects (v2) first and ruled it out for automation**: a live GraphQL call against this repo (`repository(...){ projectsV2 }`) using the exact token `git credential fill` already provides - the same one `npm run gh` uses - returned `INSUFFICIENT_SCOPES` for `read:project`; the token carries only `gist, read:org, repo, workflow`. Confirmed the repo is public now (`"private": false`, `has_issues: true`, `has_projects: true`, 0 open issues at the time), which is what makes Issues usable at all. **Chose "Issues replace the allocator" (user decision) over a Projects board (needs a manually-generated `project`-scoped PAT this session cannot create) or a fourth local-counter patch (a server-side atomic claim, sketched in D230's own header, that a SQLite file can never actually be).** `scripts/lib/deliverable-numbers.js` (D203/D226/D230) and `scripts/check-deliverable-numbers.js` deleted outright. New: `scripts/lib/github-credential.js` (token/repoSlug/redact, extracted from `gh-api.js` so the deliverable allocator doesn't duplicate D101/D218's secret handling), `scripts/lib/deliverable-issues.js` (opens a `deliverable`-labeled GitHub issue per claim; the issue number returned by GitHub's own atomic assignment IS the D-number; `createDeliverableIssuesClient` DI seam mirrors `runActor`'s `client` param in `server/apifyEquibase.js` so a check script never opens a real issue), rewritten `scripts/allocate-deliverable.js` (same `D<number>`-on-stdout contract, now backed by a real GitHub call - D218 applies here too, so it never calls `process.exit()`), and `scripts/check-deliverable-issues.js` (offline, fake-fetch verification of the new lib plus the CLI's pre-network refusals). `scripts/gh-api.js` grew symmetrical `issue <n>`/`issue list [--label]`/`issue create --title`/`issue close <n>` subcommands (2 new ALLOW entries: `POST .../issues`, `PATCH .../issues/:n`, both anchored the same way the existing `pr` entries are) and now imports `token()`/`repoSlug()` from the shared credential module instead of defining them locally. **This deliverable's own number is the proof**: claimed for real via `npm run allocate-deliverable`, landing on D324 rather than a continuation of D237 - GitHub's shared issue/PR counter had moved on by the time this could claim one (issue [#324](https://github.com/KevinRaffay/betsheet/issues/324)), which is the expected shape of the migration (gaps are free, a renumber is the real cost) rather than a defect in it. CLAUDE.md's delivery-workflow bullet and the D203/D226/D230 Gotchas entries updated (marked superseded, kept as the historical record the new mechanism's own rationale rests on); `docs/commands.md` and `docs/architecture-map.md` updated for the renamed/deleted files and the new `gh` subcommands. **VERIFIED**: `npm run check-deliverable-issues` (new, all passing, fully offline/credential-free), `npm run check-gh-api` (updated for 7 ALLOW entries and two PATCH call sites, all passing - including fixing an unrelated pre-existing test-harness bug this change exposed: the D218 fatal-injection test wrote its throw-injected copy to `os.tmpdir()`, which broke once `gh-api.js` gained a relative import, so the copy now lives beside `scripts/lib/`), `npm run build` exit 0 (no client-side changes). Real, live calls made and inspected by hand: `npm run gh -- issue 324`, `npm run allocate-deliverable -- --list`. Not automated in this deliverable: a GitHub Projects (v2) board - the user can create and manage one by hand in the web UI, or generate a `project`-scoped PAT for future automation; nothing here blocks that later. |
| D241 | House rule: the baseline app is desktop; mobile is the static app's scope | - | PR [#325](https://github.com/KevinRaffay/betsheet/pull/325), branch `claude/mobile-scope-house-rule` | merged | **User rule 2026-09-11**: *"mobile support is out of scope for baseline app, it is a desktop app and will remain so - House Rule. The only mobile support needed is in the context of the static app."* Captured in CLAUDE.md's Gotchas, immediately above D157 so the two read together, and **D157 is scoped in the same commit**: its opening line said "every UI in this codebase that a phone can reach", which a future reader could have applied to `client/`. It now says plainly that this means `static/` and only `static/`. **The rule describes what the code already does rather than asking for a change**: `client/src/styles.css` contains ZERO `@media` queries (verified by grep, 2026-09-11) while `static/src/static.css` has the 720px breakpoint D157 is about - so nothing is removed and no narrow-viewport behaviour is being changed. What changes is that a reviewer stops reading the baseline app's overflow as an open item. **Invariant 10 is why the rule is coherent rather than a preference**: the server binds 127.0.0.1, so no phone can reach the baseline app at all; the Pages payload is the only thing that leaves the machine. **The immediate consequence is in D240's own row**, which had carried the day-view table's 375px overflow as KNOWN AND NOT FIXED - it is reworded in this PR as out of scope, with the measurement kept (the pre-D240 table was already 671px at that width; a board race is 864px). Documentation only: no code, no schema, no behavior change, nothing a check script can observe. |
| D240 | Live board columns on the race card: Live rank, ML/Live Win%, Δ% and move flags | - | PR [#321](https://github.com/KevinRaffay/betsheet/pull/321), branch `claude/live-odds-move-columns` | merged | **RENUMBERED D236 -> D240 on rebase**: claimed as D236 at 05:59:39Z, and a concurrent session in a different clone merged its own D236 two minutes later (#317, 06:01:43Z) and D237 at 06:13:45Z - the residual same-window collision the allocator's own header describes, since neither claim was merged when the other was made. Loser renumbers, per the D200/D201 and D224/D225 precedent; 238/239 were skipped because that session's work is phases 1-4 and two phases were still unmerged. **User request 2026-09-11** ("Add Live Rank column to the Race Cards, and Show ML Win% and Live Win% and Delta% columns. Indicate which odds have moved a lot and could be good betting opportunities (suggest some rules)"). Four columns on every race that HAS a typed board (D232) - `Live Win%`, `Δ%`, `Live rank`, and on the shared `EntriesTable` a read-only `Live` - plus `STEAM`/`DRIFT` and `NEW FAV` tags on the horse's name. All of it is one `flagRaceEntries` call, so `RaceDayView`, `EntriesTable`, `CardView` and `ParsePreview` cannot disagree. **THE COLUMNS ARE CONDITIONAL, DERIVED FROM `comparableCount >= 2` RATHER THAN FROM A PROP**: a race with no board keeps exactly the pre-D240 table, which is what leaves the ingest preview and the whole static at-track app (no live prices in the payload, ever) untouched with no call site changed. **THE DESIGN DECISION THAT MATTERS, AND IT WAS A CORRECTION MID-BUILD**: the first cut printed the raw `1 / (odds + 1)` readings D224 established and made Δ their plain difference, which is checkable by eye and WRONG. Driving the real app showed it: a six-horse race whose ML book summed to 126% and whose typed board summed to 136% gave six deltas summing to **+10.4 points** - the gap between the books, not an opinion - so a 4/1 easing to 7/2 printed **+2.2** and read as support. A scratch does the same thing harder, re-pricing every survivor into a race-wide false STEAM. So both books are normalised over a stated BASIS first, and the pinned property is that **a race's deltas sum to zero**. The basis needs a fallback or the fix breaks the other half: over the comparable set alone, `ML Win%` blanks everywhere no board exists, so it falls back to the ML-priced live runners. `Win %` is accordingly renamed `ML Win%` and now prints the normalised share - D224's raw reading is not deleted, it moved into each cell's `title`. **THE MOVE RULE IS TWO TESTS ORed, NOT ONE**: a ratio bar (1.25x / 1.6x) is right for the long end and systematically misses the favorite, where the money is - 5/2 to 8/5 is 1.24x and +5.5 points of the whole book - while a points bar (5 / 10) never fires past about 8/1. Plus a 1-point floor on the ratio branch, because the long end of a tote is quantised: 99/1 to 60/1 is 1.5x worth 0.7 of a point and is rounding. Exported as `MOVE_THRESHOLDS` so a findings file can cite the numbers. **A flag is a LABEL, not a recommendation** - "follow the steam" and "the drifter is the value" are both real, contradictory and unmeasured here; `docs/requirements/post-time-odds-llm-comparison.md` is what answers that with an `n`, and asserting it in a tag would be concluding it by UI. **VERIFIED BY DRIVING THE REAL APP** (Playwright + the bundled Chromium, agent port 8795 against a VACUUM copy of the live corpus, never the human's pair): a board typed on Delaware Park race 1 renders all four columns with the deltas summing to zero, the tags matching the tinted cells, the saved prices in their inputs and the raw readings in the tooltips; race 2 on the same page, with no board, renders the pre-D240 ten columns. Both themes, `#root` intact, 0 page errors, 0 failed requests. Screenshots read, not just measured. **check-entry-flags 53 -> 85 assertions with SIX negative controls, and the controls earned their keep twice**: one proved the scratch fixture was not the exact rescale its comment claimed (5/1,8/1,12/1 -> 2/1,3/1,5/1 is not proportional in probability space; it is now constructed, and the assertion is zero to 12 decimal places rather than under a threshold), and one passed green while live-rank ties were broken, because no fixture had a tie - a tote prints in buckets, so two horses at 7/2 is ordinary. 43 of 44 check scripts green **including `check-static-app`**; `check-equibase-otr` needs Poppler and fails on `main` too. **OUT OF SCOPE, and D241 made that a rule**: at 375px the day-view table overflows and the Live input is not reachable without sideways scrolling. This was flagged here as a known-not-fixed item; the user's house rule of 2026-09-11 (D241) settles it as out of scope instead - the baseline app is a desktop app and mobile is `static/`'s job. It was never D157's rule either (that binds the phone-reachable static app, and this server is loopback-only, invariant 10), and it PREDATES this change: the pre-D240 table already measured 671px at a 375px viewport. D240 widens a board race to 864px. **CLAUDE.md gains both gotchas.** |
| D237 | Extract `CardSheet.jsx` as a pure, prop-driven component from `CardView.jsx` (phase 2 of 4) | 4 | PR [#319](https://github.com/KevinRaffay/betsheet/pull/319), branch `claude/ecstatic-mayer-up4s0g` | merged | **Phase 2 of the static-app read-only rework** (D236, phase 1) - a pure refactor with no behavior change, done ahead of the multi-day payload/viewer work (phase 3) because that phase needs a sheet renderer that takes card data as a prop rather than fetching by id. Everything in `CardView.jsx`'s render **after** the `{!embedded && <pagehead>}` block (info line, over-bankroll warning, day-summary footer, collapse-all buttons, per-race `<details>` blocks, multi-race tickets, `FAILURE_MODE_WARNINGS`, the responsible-gambling line) plus the pure helpers (`money`/`pct`/`estDisplay`/`splitThesis`/`RaceResults`/`TicketRow`/`GradeSubtotal`) moved into new `client/src/components/CardSheet.jsx`, taking `{card, races, results, notesByRace, grades}` as props. `CardView.jsx` shrinks to the `getCard`/`getGrades`/`getLlmNotes` fetches, `handleGrade`/`handleDelete`, the `!embedded` pagehead, and renders `<CardSheet .../>`. **Two refactors made DURING extraction, not incidental to it**, so the sheet never depends on raw SQLite row ids a payload has no business carrying: single-vs-multi-race ticket grouping switched from `t.race_id != null` to `t.selections.races.length === 1 && t.selections.races[0] === raceNumber` (every ticket's `selections` JSON already carries `races`, confirmed in `server/human-cards.js`); the ticket-to-grade join switched from `t.id`/`g.ticket_id` to `t.sequence`/`g.sequence` (unique per card, present on every ticket and every graded-ticket row per `server/grading.js`'s own query), used as the React list key too. `card.llmModelLabel` is now a pre-resolved prop (`../api.js`'s `modelLabel()`, called by `CardView.jsx`) rather than the raw `llm_model` id - `CardSheet.jsx` must never import `api.js`, a fetch module, just to reach a pure string table. **VERIFIED**: `npm run build` exit 0; a real graded HUMAN card seeded via `insertRaceDay`/`persistHumanRace`/direct `race_results` inserts/`gradeAndPersist` (mirroring the check scripts' own fixture pattern, no engine involved), served from the agent's `dev:preview` stack (never the human's port pair) and driven with Playwright at `/card/:id`: pagehead, HUMAN chip, bankroll/day-total math, graded summary, WIN/LOSS outcome tags and P/L figures (+$64/−$12/+$52 race 1, −$10 race 2) all rendered correctly and exactly matched the seeded data, screenshotted and read. `embedded` mode (used by `ReplayDayLanding.jsx`) is unchanged code-path-wise (same `{!embedded && ...}` conditional, untouched) and was not separately re-verified in a browser - its only difference from the tested standalone path is the omitted pagehead block. |
| D235 | Editable fields look editable: a consistent form-control affordance | - | PR [#315](https://github.com/KevinRaffay/betsheet/pull/315), branch `claude/editable-field-affordance` | merged | **User rule 2026-09-11**: *"it is not clear if the live odds fields are editable - there is no border or contrast and they look like labels. I shouldn't have to hover a field to indicate that is an update. Apply a consistent non-ambiguous style."* **The report was about the odds field; the defect was global.** `.in` was `border: 1px solid transparent; background: transparent` with the border revealed only on `:hover` - a label until you touch it, and on a touch device a label permanently. **The stylesheet already contradicted itself**: `.formrow input` and `.pastebox textarea` both carried a real border and background, so one concept had two opposite styles and the newest fields got the wrong one. **Fixed on the ELEMENTS, not the class** - `input:not([type=checkbox]):not([type=radio]):not([type=file])`, `select`, `textarea` now carry border, field background, radius, padding and focus ring, and `.in` is layout only. That choice is the point: **7 text-ish controls in this app carry no class at all** (two date inputs, two number inputs, four selects, a textarea), so a class-based fix would have left them ambiguous and the next unclassed field would regress again. New tokens `--color-field-bg`/`--color-field-border` in both themes, the border at Radix **step 7** (the "UI element border" step) rather than step 6, which is for dividers and reads as decoration on a control. Checkboxes, radios and file inputs are excluded - native affordance, and a text-field box around them reads as a mistake. `:focus-visible` gets a 3px accent ring; the old rule set `outline: none` and tinted a 1px border, which is an accessibility problem as much as a clarity one. The duplicated border/background/colour/radius declarations were removed from `.formrow` and `.pastebox`, which now keep only their own padding/sizing. **VERIFIED BY DRIVING THE REAL APP** (Playwright + the bundled Chromium, against a VACUUM copy of the live corpus on the agent's own port 8795, never the human's pair): `getComputedStyle` read on every control across **four routes** (`/day/:id`, `/new`, `/sources`, `/calendar`, `/pl`) and **both themes** - **0 borderless fields anywhere**, light border `rgb(208,205,215)` (mauve-7) on `rgb(253,252,253)`, dark `rgb(73,71,78)` on `rgb(18,17,19)`, focus ring present, `#root` intact and **0 console errors** on every page. Screenshots taken and read, not just measured - which is how the second defect was spotted. **A SECOND DEFECT THE NEW CLARITY EXPOSED**: D232 set the live-odds placeholder to the horse's morning line. On a ghost field that read as a faint hint; on a real field it reads as **a value already entered**, and the M/L is in the adjacent column anyway. Now `"odds"`. 43 of 44 check scripts green **including `check-static-app`** - `client/src/styles.css` IS in the static app's import surface, so the static builder gets the same affordance; `check-equibase-otr` needs Poppler and fails on `main` too. **CLAUDE.md gains the house rule.** |
| D236 | Static app: remove card-construction functionality, strip to a read-only shell (phase 1 of 4) | 4 | PR [#317](https://github.com/KevinRaffay/betsheet/pull/317), branch `claude/ecstatic-mayer-up4s0g` | merged | **User request 2026-09-11**: retire the at-track ticket-builder surface (D150-D158) in favor of a read-only viewer over the corpus - race calendar, race days, race cards including graded P&L - with no inputs, no local storage of picks, no Build/Export/Forget actions, superseding the 2026-09-08 "not a current concern" scope note in `docs/feature-status.md`. Phase 1 of 4: this deliverable is the removal only. **Deleted**: `static/src/CardsView.jsx`, `ReferenceCards.jsx`, `card.js`, `exchange.js`, `storage.js` (the IndexedDB draft store), `shared/static-export.js`, `scripts/import-static-cards.js`, `scripts/check-static-import.js`. **Stripped**: `app.jsx` (storage gate, `deviceId`, the `unexported:N` counter, `beforeunload` guard, all removed; kept the hash router and `loadPayload`/`validateStaticPayload`), `DayView.jsx` (dropped the card picker, "Start a new card", the per-card State column; the action link is now an unconditional View), `RaceView.jsx` (dropped `TicketBuilder`, the lock/PASS/discard state machine and the render-time committed-text snapshot D155 built - there is no ticket being composed here any more). **The payload/builder are deliberately UNCHANGED** - `shared/static-payload.js`, `scripts/build-static-payload.js`, `scripts/check-static-payload.js`, and the committed `static/public/payload.json` all stay on the existing v3, one-race-day, `--reference-cards` shape; mixing a breaking schema redesign into a pure-subtraction phase would scope-mix two independently reviewable changes into one. That redesign (multi-day bundling, unconditional cards with grades) is a later phase. **`scripts/check-static-app.js` needed more than the D157 `views` array trim this phase's own plan first assumed**: its parser-round-trip section fed payload data through the real `parseHumanPicksText`/`estimateTicketPayouts` to prove the app validated a ticket the same way the server would - a claim that stopped being true the moment ticket construction left the app, so the section is removed rather than deferred. Its two bundle-exclusion POSITIVE controls (`EXACTA BOX`/`SUPERFECTA`, reachable only through the now-deleted `card.js`→`shared/parsers/human-picks.js` path, and `betsheet-static`, `storage.js`'s own deleted IndexedDB name) would have failed the moment this phase's deletions landed, not later - replaced with a new positive control (`pagehead--static`, a literal in `app.jsx`'s surviving header markup) and two new negative controls proving the removed construction surface stays removed (`wagerLimitsFor`/`comboCountFor` from `human-picks.js`, and the literal `betsheet-static` cache/store name itself, now checked as FORBIDDEN rather than required). **Migration 027 is left as-is** (`cards.external_id`/`built_on`/`saw_reference_cards`) - shipped migrations are immutable in this codebase, and a new migration to drop three now-unused nullable columns from the ten-child `cards` table isn't worth the churn for zero functional gain; already-imported HUMAN cards are unaffected, ordinary corpus rows now. **CLAUDE.md**: rewrote the "one deliberate exception, D150-D155" paragraph - the static app no longer adds cards to the corpus, so it is no longer an exception to the Phase 1-3-before-4 sequencing rule, just an ordinary read-only reporting surface; marked the D155 render-time-snapshot Gotcha HISTORICAL (mechanism removed, the React lesson kept, same treatment the repo already gives engine-era invariants); re-derived the D168 "seven places" list, which drops from seven to three (`EntriesTable.jsx`, `client/src/styles.css`, `shared/static-payload.js`) now that `TicketBuilder.jsx`/`human-picks.js`/`betmath.js`/`static-export.js` have no remaining direct import edge from `static/src/`. `docs/feature-status.md`'s 2026-09-08 scope note marked superseded. `docs/trace-schema.md`'s `static_card_imported` event marked historical (nothing emits it any more; past log entries with it remain valid). `docs/commands.md`/`docs/architecture-map.md` updated for the dropped scripts/files and the app's new shape. `.github/workflows/deploy-pages.yml` drops `TicketBuilder.jsx` from its trigger paths and its header comments no longer call the app a "ticket builder". `package.json` drops the `check-static-import`/`import-static-cards` script entries. **VERIFIED**: `npm run check-static-payload` (unmodified, exit 0 - the payload/builder didn't change), `npm run check-static-app` (rewritten, exit 0), `npm run build:static` (exit 0), and a browser pass at `:5187` confirming the day and race views render read-only with no construction UI, no console errors, and D157/D158's mobile mechanics (col-detail hiding, entries collapsed on a phone, no rank column, no conditions blurb) all intact. |
| D234 | Label LLM cards by whether they saw the live board | - | PR [#313](https://github.com/KevinRaffay/betsheet/pull/313), branch `claude/label-live-odds-cards` | merged | **User request 2026-09-11** ("label morning vs post-time cards so they're separable"), raised because D232/D233 made a race able to carry two LLM cards that every aggregate would pool - the same failure invariant 13 names, one level down: two different experiments reported as one number. **THE LABEL IS "SAW THE BOARD", NOT "POST TIME"**, and the difference is not pedantry: a card generated at 4pm with nobody having typed the board has a prompt BYTE-IDENTICAL to the morning one, because D233 renders the LIVE field only when a price exists. The clock is not the variable; the information is. **Stored, not derived from the prompt text.** D221's `llmInputsLabel` greps the stored prompt because that input was never recorded anywhere else; this project's preference when it HAS the choice is `cards.notes_present` (D92), a column. A grep for `· LIVE ` would bet on a rendering detail in a template whose own history (D112/D125/D136/D145/D160...) is a record of changing repeatedly. Migration 037 adds `live_odds_present` to BOTH `cards` and `llm_card_requests`, mirroring notes exactly: the request row is the per-race truth, the card flag LATCHES ("at least one race saw a board") for D92's own reason - a person prices the 3 races they are betting out of 8, so a flag meaning "every race" reads false on every real card. Surfaced in P/L as a `byLiveOdds` split (`Saw the live board` / `Morning line only`, mirroring `byNotes`), a `board` tag on the card row, and in `/sources` as part of the group key (`claude-sonnet-5 [none +board]`). **THE REAL FIND, and labelling alone would not have fixed it**: D221's dedupe keeps the NEWEST card per (day, model, race), which was right while a second card could only be a REGENERATION - the same experiment redone, D28's append-only rule. D233 made a second card possibly a DIFFERENT experiment, so the old key SILENTLY DISCARDED the morning card and reported the post-time one as though it were the only card that ever existed - worse than pooling, which at least shows both. The board is now part of the dedupe key, read per race from the request row (the card flag latches and would mislabel a race that never had a board). Caught by the assertion written for the split, which found one group where there should have been two. **VERIFIED**: `check-pick-scoring-api` gains the two-cards-one-race probe (different groups, each carrying only its own race, per-race rows saying which saw a board); `check-llm-cards` gains six D234 assertions; `check-schema` two. **A NEGATIVE CONTROL FOUND A REAL GAP**: forcing `liveOddsPresent: false` at the call site broke NOTHING, because nothing asserted the flag was ever set - a wrong wiring would have labelled every card morning-line forever and the split would have read one-sided. It now fails 5 assertions. A second control (board out of the dedupe key) fails 5. Three existing expectations were UPDATED, not relaxed - they enumerate every group and count cards per bucket, and the probe legitimately adds two groups; the persisting block also moved to the end of `check-llm-cards` because it shifted per-model card counts that were themselves correct. Migration 037 on a COPY of the real corpus: 231 cards and 420 requests all label as morning-line, which is right - there was no board to see before D232. 43 of 44 green; `check-equibase-otr` needs Poppler and fails on `main` too. |
| D232 | Manual live odds entry, per race, per runner | - | PR [#311](https://github.com/KevinRaffay/betsheet/pull/311), branch `claude/manual-live-odds` | merged | **User request 2026-09-11**, after the user challenged D228's premise: *"Are you sure equibase entries have live odds before the race?"* **They do not, and the check settled it.** The `LiveOdds` column is EMPTY in the served HTML - 123 of 123 cells in this repo's own Del Mar fixture - with `title="Live Odds refreshed every 60 seconds"` on the header and an external `/js/liveOdds.js` filling the cells client-side. Both capture shapes this codebase supports are the ORIGINAL SERVER MARKUP by `equibase-entries.js`'s own definition, so both are empty at any hour; the requirements doc's "this capture predates wagering" was the wrong diagnosis. The Apify route fails for the same reason - the actor scrapes that page server-side, and all three real datasets carry `morningLineOdds` and no live-odds field at all. **So the board is typed.** New `reconcileManualOdds` in `shared/live-odds.js`, the sibling of D228's `reconcileOddsCapture` and deliberately the same shape, making the same four promises: never the morning line, never the entry list, never a scratch, never an unmatched race. A partial board is NORMAL and unwarned - at a track you price the horses you are betting. New `POST /api/race-days/:id/races/:n/live-odds`. **Per race in its TIMING too** (user decision): each save stamps its own `captured_at`, and the route deliberately does NOT touch `race_days.odds_captured_at`, which is one-per-day by construction (D116/D117) - a per-race time written there would make the day-level staleness indicator claim a freshness no race has. Migration 036 widens `odds_captures.source` to admit `'manual'` (CHECK rebuild, the 033 shape); D228's capture-HISTORY storage is reused unchanged, because the drift between two boards is still the signal. UI: an editable odds cell beside each horse's M/L in the race panel (user decision: a field per runner, not a paste box), per the house rule that a race-specific input belongs in the Race UI. `RaceDayView` renders its OWN entries table, so `EntriesTable.jsx` and the static app are untouched; the one new CSS class is feature-named and was grepped first (D216). **VERIFIED**: `check-live-odds` 60 -> 86 assertions, all green. **TWO REAL BUGS FOUND BY THE NEW TESTS.** (1) `readOdds('  ')` returned `{text:'', decimal:null}` - truthy - so a typed space stored an EMPTY price and counted as one; `blank()` now trims. The UI filters blanks before sending, but a reconciler relying on its caller to have done that has a hole in it. (2) The route called `now()`, which is not defined in that module - a ReferenceError on every request omitting `oddsCapturedAt`, **which is exactly what the UI sends**, so every real save would have 500'd. The happy-path test passed an explicit time and never reached it; the refusal tests did, which is how it surfaced. Both are now pinned by named assertions. End-to-end on a real server: the typed board saves with its own time and `source='manual'`, race 2 is untouched (it is per race), `race_days.odds_captured_at` is provably not moved, an empty board is 422, an unknown race 404, and the history names which race a capture covered. Migration 036 applied to a COPY of the real corpus: 169 days / 13,710 entries / 231 cards / 3,966 grades intact, `integrity_check` ok, 0 FK violations. 43 of 44 check scripts green including `check-static-app` (`client/src/styles.css` IS in the static import surface); `check-equibase-otr` needs Poppler and fails on `main` too. **CLAUDE.md gained the finding as a gotcha** - a column existing in the markup is not the same as a column carrying data. |
| D233 | Live odds in the LLM prompt, conditionally | - | PR [#311](https://github.com/KevinRaffay/betsheet/pull/311), branch `claude/manual-live-odds` | merged | The other half of the user's post-time workflow, and the blocker that would have made it pointless: D228/D232 store the board, but `buildLlmRaceUserPrompt` printed `- ML 5/2` and nothing else, so a post-time regeneration would have run a BYTE-IDENTICAL prompt and merely resampled the morning-line answer. An entry line now appends `· LIVE 9/5` when that horse has a price, and `buildSystemPrompt({hasLiveOdds})` adds a `LIVE ODDS` clause block. **CONDITIONAL, and that is the load-bearing part**: LLM cards have no version axis - `engine_version` is the literal string `'llm'` for every one of them - so an unconditional field would have silently changed the prompt for every future odds-free card too and made it incomparable with the 293 already stored. A race with no live odds renders byte-identically to pre-D233, asserted directly. Same pattern D92's notes clauses and D179's baseline clauses already use. **What the clause block actually says matters as much as that it exists**: the naive reading of a live price is "bet the favourite", which is a reliably losing strategy after 15-20% takeout, so the block names the DRIFT between morning line and board as the signal, says plainly that agreeing with the market is not the goal, tells the model to price the bet against the live number, and warns that a horse with no live price is not a horse nobody bet - a partial board is normal and the absence is not information. `hasLiveOdds` is true only when a LIVE horse carries a price, so a fully-scratched or unpriced race is unaffected. `loadRace` already `SELECT *`s, so reaching the column took one line - the data has been sitting there since migration 024 and the prompt simply never read it. **VERIFIED** as part of `check-live-odds`'s 86: the byte-identical no-odds system prompt, the opt-in block, the odds-free entry line unchanged, the priced line, a partial board pricing only the horse that has one, and the two clauses that keep it from being a chalk machine. `check-llm-cards` green (it asserts the notes-free prompt is byte-identical to `SYSTEM_PROMPT`, which this had to not break). |

All 38 are therefore `merged`; **no row was closed-unmerged, so no new Status value was needed** and none was invented (the vocabulary stays planned / in progress / in review / merged).
